## Overview

Dr. Adnan H. Siddiqui is a neurosurgeon in Amherst, New York and is affiliated with [Kaleida Health-Buffalo General Medical Center](https://health.usnews.com/best-hospitals/area/ny/buffalo-general-medical-center-6211025). He received his medical degree from Aga Khan Medical College and has been in practice for more than 20 years. Dr. Adnan H. Siddiqui accepts Medicare, Aetna, Cigna, Blue Cross - see other [insurance plans accepted](https://health.usnews.com/doctors/adnan-siddiqui-318109#insurance). Dr. Adnan H. Siddiqui is highly recommended by patients.

## Specialties

Specialty

### Neurosurgery

Neurosurgeons use minimally invasive surgery to treat brain, nervous system and spinal nerve disorders. Conditions treated by neurosurgeons include brain bleeds, head or spinal cord trauma or tumors.
